# P0-11 Resume-ability: the plan, a root README, progress and decisions logs in the repository

## Spec

What: make the repository self-describing, so that a person or a fresh Claude session can open it after a break and learn, from committed files alone, what Bridle is, what the plan is, what has shipped, what is in flight, what is next, which decisions the owner made and what only the owner can do. Today the approved plan lives only in `~/.claude/plans/` on one machine, there is no root README, several owner decisions exist only in chat, and the progress record is spread over 70 cards and the monitor.

Why: the user's instruction of 2026-11-03 ("prioritize this so that we will not hallucinate"): a session that cannot find the state will invent it. The committed copy becomes the canonical plan; every later amendment is a commit.

Acceptance criteria:
1. `docs/plan/roadmap.md` is the approved plan, copied verbatim from `/Users/johnstephenf.garcia/.claude/plans/lets-plan-first-in-stateless-pizza.md` (Parts A to F, the context, the approval section and the decisions table), with one header block prepended: approved 2026-11-03 (second approval), canonical from this card's commit, amendments are commits to this file, the original path. `docs/plan/monitor-v4.md` is the same for `/Users/johnstephenf.garcia/.claude/plans/can-we-plan-on-floofy-forest.md` (the monitor v4 plan of card P0-10). Nothing in either body is edited, summarized or "improved".
2. A root `README.md` ("start here", one screen): what Bridle is (two or three sentences from the plan's context), the repository map (docs/requirements, docs/architecture, docs/ux, docs/decisions, docs/ops, docs/plan, factory, tools, packages, crates: one line each), where the state is (`python3 tools/monitor/card.py list`, `/factory status`, `docs/plan/progress.md`, the monitor link from `.bridle/monitor.json`, the local viewer), how to resume (`/factory next`, the preconditions in `.claude/skills/factory/SKILL.md`), the by-hand items (a link to the roadmap's E4 table and to the open items in the progress log), and the rules for several sessions in one checkout. Every relative link resolves.
3. `docs/plan/progress.md`: (a) a board section between the markers `<!-- board:start -->` and `<!-- board:end -->`, generated by a new `python3 tools/monitor/card.py status --write docs/plan/progress.md` from the cards (one table: id, phase, station, status, owner, updated; then "next up"; no timestamp or other field that changes between two identical runs), with `--check docs/plan/progress.md` exiting 1 and naming the file when the section is stale and 0 otherwise; (b) a hand-written log with one dated entry per shipped card so far (PLAN-01 to PLAN-05, P0-01, P0-03, P0-04, P0-10, P0-06.1 to P0-06.3, P0-02.1 to P0-02.3), each naming the commit hash taken from the card's `## Ship` section and verified against `git log` (never from memory), what it delivered in one line, and a current "In flight / next / only the owner can do" block (P0-02.4 at test, this card, P0-02.5 and P0-02.6 next, then P0-08 per the owner's decision; the open E4 items: design partners and two SME reviewers and the spring-2028 term before P1-05, the AWS account before the P0-05 apply sub-card, the Anthropic key before P5-03).
4. `docs/decisions/log.md`: a dated table (date, decision, who, where it is applied or recorded) of the owner's operational decisions, each entry citing the card or doc that already records it, or marked "chat only; this log is the record": the monitor built before the rest of planning and before any product code (P0-01); the two-stage plan approval, Part D first and the complete plan second, with ADR 0002 adopted at the second (PLAN-05, commit ce7b2dc); the four answers at the second approval: the factory installs the toolchain itself, the factory may push `main` and open pull requests on github.com/stephenfgarcia/Bridle, the P0-06 split, and the lanes card P0-08 right after P0-02 (P0-06 and P0-06.3 cards; the lanes answer is chat only); the repository private first and then public to get branch protection on the free plan (P0-06.3 Spec, docs/ops/github.md); the factory may merge its own pull requests, the owner's rule `Bash(gh pr merge *)` (docs/ops/permissions.md, P0-02.3 Ship); the monitor mirror polling every 5 s and the local viewer, liveness over token cost (P0-10 if recorded there, else chat only); the deferrals adopted with ADR 0002 (Diagnose tasks, the similarity report, the self-serve sandbox); the UX defaults adopted at approval (roadmap C8: memo weight 15, reference values withheld until due, TA grants accept and escalate only, tolerance band visible, tenant prefix `/o/:org`, formative tasks not instructor-reviewed); the foreman's amendments the owner was told of (Postgres on host port 55432 and RustFS in place of MinIO, P0-02.2); and the decisions still open (design partners, SME reviewers, pilot term, AWS account). The ADRs stay the record for architecture; the log links them. No entry is invented: an entry the builder cannot source to a card, a doc or this Spec is left out.
5. `factory/README.md` gains a "Resume" section (read the root README, `card.py list`, the progress log, the roadmap E3; then `/factory next`) and a "Several sessions at once" section (re-read a file before editing it, stage only your own files with explicit paths and never `git add -A`, announce commits to the other session, only one session runs the watch); `factory/line.md`'s ship exit criteria add "the progress board regenerated and the log entry written"; `.claude/skills/factory/SKILL.md` step 0 reads the roadmap from `docs/plan/roadmap.md` (not the `~/.claude/plans` file) and step 6 regenerates the board and appends the log entry before the ship commit.
6. `tools/test/docs.test.sh` (bash 3.2, the shape of the other `tools/test/*.test.sh`): README links resolve; every card id in the roadmap's E2 table has a card file and every non-sub-card implementation card is an E2 row (sub-cards `<row>.<n>` and the tooling cards P0-01, P0-03, P0-04, P0-10, P0-11 and PLAN-01 to PLAN-05 are exempt), with dependsOn or estimate differences printed as `# warning:` lines, not failures; `card.py status --check` passes on the committed `docs/plan/progress.md`; the decisions log has at least the entries of AC4 by keyword. The test runs as a step of `tools/check.sh` (so `pnpm check` and CI's code job run it); the step count in its output and every test that pins that count are updated.

Files: README.md (new), docs/plan/roadmap.md (new), docs/plan/monitor-v4.md (new), docs/plan/progress.md (new), docs/decisions/log.md (new), factory/README.md, factory/line.md, .claude/skills/factory/SKILL.md, tools/monitor/card.py (status), tools/monitor/test/check.test.py or a new card.py test, tools/test/docs.test.sh (new), tools/check.sh, and any test pinning the check step count.

Out of scope: exporting the monitor's events into git (the cards are the durable record; `tools/monitor/sync.md` covers replay); changes to the monitor page or the mirror; restructuring `docs/`; editing the plan's content; CI's `docs` job (P0-09 may add the docs test there); product code.

Process note (foreman, 2026-11-03): the owner prioritized this card, so it runs alongside P0-02.4's test and review stations, an exception to one card at a time. The two cards share no files: P0-02.4 owns `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `tools/entrypoint.sh`, `docs/ops/local-dev.md`, `tools/test/image.test.sh` and its own card; this card's stations must not touch them, and `tools/agent-hq/` belongs to another session. Every station: stage nothing, commit nothing; the foreman ships. An independent audit of the repository's resume-ability (three readers and a critic) is running; its findings are appended to this Spec as an addendum if they arrive before the build, else sent to the builder.

Audit addendum (foreman, 2026-11-03 14:55Z, from the independent resume-ability audit: three read-only readers and a critic; the builder reads this before writing the README, the progress log, the decisions log and the SKILL; the tester and the reviewer hold the card to it):
- AC1, header block: it also states that Part D sections D2 to D6 describe monitor v1 to v3 and are superseded by `docs/plan/monitor-v4.md` and `tools/monitor/README.md` (protocol v2, 2026-11-03), and that the body's status statements (Context, D0, "PLAN-05 sits at review", E4 rows 1 and 2) are as presented on 2026-11-03 07:23 while `docs/plan/progress.md` is the live status. The in-repo pointers to the plan file change to `docs/plan/roadmap.md`: `docs/ops/secrets.md` ("E4 of the plan"), `docs/architecture/build-sequence.md` ("Part E of the plan"), `docs/requirements/00-index.md` ("the approved plan (Part A)"), and `.claude/skills/factory/SKILL.md` (plan step 9).
- AC3 and AC6, the critic's blocking finding: the board carries station, owner and updated, which change at every `card.py move`, while the board is regenerated only at ship; a strict `--check` inside `tools/check.sh` would therefore fail at every station of every card and in every other session's `pnpm check`. So `--check` compares only two sets between the board and the cards: the card ids, and the ids with `status: done` (the done set changes only at ship, the id set only at `card.py new`); station, owner, updated and the next-up line are rendered by `--write` and not checked. The board is regenerated at every `card.py new` and at every ship. The docs test's negative proof flips a `done` cell to `planned` (or removes a row) in the mktemp copy; `card.test.py` covers both sets and a station-only change that must pass.
- AC3, the owner block: a table, one row per E4 row of the roadmap (nine) plus the six M0 items of `docs/architecture/architecture.md` section 17 (counsel engaged, AISC Shapes and ACI requests sent, SME framework agreements, design partners named, spring-2028 term confirmed, contractor R2 start); columns: item, needed before (card), due (M0 2026-11-16 where it applies), status (done with the card that proves it: the installs P0-06.1; the push, Actions and branch protection P0-06.3 and P0-02.3; otherwise open; otherwise unknown). Sources: roadmap E4, architecture.md section 17, `docs/ops/secrets.md` ("Obtained at" column), `docs/ops/github.md`. The in-flight block is the state at build time (this card only; P0-02.4 shipped in `d8d8b7e`). The log also carries P0-06 (work package closed in `dfd0b72`) and P0-02.4, and one sentence that cards shipped before `0e2e2cf` predate the Actions workflow and carry no run URL.
- AC4: the rows for "Part D first" and "P0-08 right after P0-02" cite `docs/plan/roadmap.md` (the approval section's first-approval sentence and decision 3; E3), not "chat only"; one row per plan-approval decision 1 to 5 with its default, marked "default in force, not yet answered" for 1 (design partners, SME reviewers, term) and 4 (the AWS and provider accounts); the P0-02.2 amendments are the plan station's, the owner was told.
- AC2: one line of the repository map says `tools/agent-hq/` is another session's untracked standalone app (excluded from Biome by `049b0fd`), not lost factory work, without a link; one sentence under "where the state is": the per-tool event log is machine-local (`.bridle/progress/`) and in the claude.ai artifact; the cards and git are the durable record.
- AC5: SKILL.md step 6 says the shipped card state is committed at once as `Cards: <id> shipped` together with the regenerated board (not "with the next card"); step 1 says the new card file is committed as `Cards: <id> spec` once the spec is approved; the one-card-at-a-time rule gains: a second card may run alongside only when the owner says so and the two share no files, recorded in the card's Spec as a process note. `factory/line.md`'s ship row adds that a phase exit is a dated entry in `docs/plan/progress.md`. `.claude/agents/factory-planner.md` and `.claude/agents/factory-tester.md` each gain one sentence with the E1 fixtures-in-plan rule, quoted from `docs/plan/roadmap.md` E1.
- Stale lines fixed in this card, one line each: `docs/requirements/08-product-definition-personas-mvp-scope.md` MVP-02 gains "Superseded by ADR 0001 R1 and ADR 0002 A2/A6: AISC 360-22 LRFD W-shapes only; RC members in Analyze tasks only; ASD after the pilot", and `docs/decisions/0002-architecture-amendments.md`'s list of amended rows in file 08 adds MVP-02; `docs/ux/README.md`'s MVP screen count agrees with `docs/ux/screen-inventory.md` (count the inventory's rows and fix whichever is wrong); `docs/ops/secrets.md`'s sentence that the `.env` patterns "are not there yet" becomes "present in `.gitignore` since P0-02.1 (`bd758ed`)".
- Done by the foreman, not the builder: the orders P0-08 3, P0-07 4, P0-05 5 (owner decision 3: lanes right after P0-02, so `card.py list` names P0-08 next once P0-02 closes), committed and mirrored; the empty `## Ship` sections of P0-01 (`9b95026`, `ecc7870`), P0-06 (its sub-cards' commits) and PLAN-05 (`ce7b2dc`) filled at this card's ship; this card committed as `Cards: P0-11 spec and plan` during the build.

Correction and decision (foreman, 2026-11-04, after the review FAIL): (1) the audit addendum's wording for `docs/ops/secrets.md` ("present in `.gitignore` since P0-02.1 (`bd758ed`)") was wrong and came from the foreman: `git log -S'.env.local' -- .gitignore` shows the two patterns arrived in `89bed6d` (PLAN-04: Adversarial polish follow-ups), the same commit that wrote the old "not there yet" sentence; `bd758ed` added only the build-output block. The line reads "present in `.gitignore` since `89bed6d` (PLAN-04 follow-ups)". (2) The reviewer's advisory on the ship order is adopted: in SKILL.md step 6 the ship commit carries the code and the card through `## Review`; then `## Ship` gets the hash and the run URL, `card.py move <id> ship --status done`, `card.py status --write docs/plan/progress.md`, and the log entry with its hash and time, all in the `Cards: <id> shipped` commit, pushed at once. So no commit ever holds a log entry without a hash, and every commit passes `pnpm check` in a full clone.

The builder fixes exactly the items below; the direction is fixed here, the wording comes from the primary source named, and no fix
may replace a wrong statement with a different unsourced one. The details and proofs are in `## Test`, second pass.
1. F1 and F8, `README.md` "How to resume" step 2 and `factory/README.md` "## Resume": a parked or in-flight card comes first. If
   `python3 tools/monitor/card.py list` shows a card between spec and review (running or blocked), `/factory run <id>` as the pause
   note at the end of its Spec says; otherwise `/factory next` (`card.py` `next_up` takes backlog cards only).

   audit addendum give: the board is regenerated at every `card.py new` and at every ship; station moves in between are rendered at
   the next regeneration and not checked. `README.md` (the fifth several-sessions rule) and `docs/plan/progress.md` (the last
   sentence of its opening paragraph) say that and nothing wider.
3. F3, `README.md` repository map: the `tools/test` label says what the files are (one test file per shipped P0-02 and P0-06
   sub-card, plus `docs.test.sh` for P0-11; the monitor's tests are in `tools/monitor/test`), from the headers of those files.
4. F4, `docs/plan/progress.md` owner table, E4.7: status `unknown` with the reason (no card or document records the runners), as the
   table's own legend defines it.
5. F6, `docs/decisions/log.md` intro: a decision is either sourced to a card, a document or the P0-11 Spec, or marked "chat only;
   this log is the record"; nothing is invented. The six rows of `8cdcf34` stay as they are.

   body changes; `docs/plan/monitor-v4.md`'s origin still holds its plan (109 lines, 21,663 bytes) and its header stays.
9. F7, ruled in as wording in SKILL.md only (the `new` command and step 0): the mirror write uses the artifact database tool the
   session offers. Where the harness has `mcp__claude-artifacts__db_write`, call it with `slug`, `ops` and `idempotencyKey` from the
   line. Where it has `ArtifactData` instead (this session, 2026-11-17: the foreman mirrored the whole second test station with
   it), one `batch` call per printed line with the `url` from `.bridle/monitor.json`, each op
   `{"op":"set","path":"events/<id>","data":{..}}` becoming the write `{op: "set", collection: "events", doc_id: "<id>", data}`
   (for `tasks/<id>`: collection `tasks`); it takes no idempotency key, and a create of an id that already exists is refused, which
   `mirror.py ack <batch> --exists` already handles. `tools/monitor/sync.md` and `tools/monitor/ops.sh` carry the old tool name and
   belong to the monitor's owner session: this card does not touch them; the foreman reports them to the owner as a follow-up.

Constraints from the monitor's owner session (via the foreman, 2026-11-03): (1) `card.py status` emits no monitor event at all; the board is derived from the cards, so nothing needs reporting. If an event were ever wanted it would go through `tools/monitor/log.sh` or `monlib.append_event`, never a hand-written line, so the id and seq rules hold (`mirror.py` relies on them, `check.py` validates the tree). (2) Nothing is written under `.bridle/progress/` (the mirror scans that tree): the only output of `--write` is the file named on the command line, `docs/plan/progress.md`; `--check` writes nothing anywhere.

   - `card.py status [--cards DIR] [--write FILE | --check FILE]`; `--write`/`--check` in an `add_mutually_exclusive_group()`; `--cards` defaults to `CARDS` (as `serve.py --cards` does) so the test can point it at a temporary directory.

### Tests, one per acceptance criterion, and the commands
AC1 → `bash tools/test/docs.test.sh` case `plan-copies`, plus the two `diff` commands of steps 1 and 2 run by hand (output pasted in `## Test`). AC2 → case `readme`. AC3 → case `board`, and `python3 tools/monitor/test/card.test.py` (4 tests) for the `--write`/`--check` contract on fixtures. AC4 → case `decisions`. AC5 → case `factory-docs`. AC6 → case `roadmap-cards`, then `pnpm check` (expect `check: 7/7 docs ok` and `check: 7/7 ok in <t>s`) and `bash tools/test/workspace.test.sh` (case `check`: seven step lines, fail-fast at `1/7 lint FAILED` and `4/7 cargo-fmt FAILED`; about two minutes, needs the toolchain prefix). Regression: `python3 tools/monitor/test/serve.test.py` (serve.py loads card.py's `parse`/`task_doc`), `python3 tools/monitor/test/check.test.py`, `python3 tools/monitor/test/mirror.test.py`; `git status --porcelain` identical before and after `pnpm check`; `find tools -name __pycache__` empty.

### Risks and how to check them
- Verbatim copy: the roadmap source lacks a final newline, so a byte-for-byte `cmp` against the raw source fails by one byte; the check compares against the source plus one newline (step 1), and the test pins 371/109 body lines, the H1 and the last line. Any later amendment to the plan changes those pins on purpose, in the same commit.
- Stale board: every `card.py move` (P0-02.4 ships alongside; this card's own moves) makes `--check` fail until `--write` runs. The tester regenerates the board if a card moved under it and says so in `## Test`; the foreman regenerates at every commit that touches a card (line.md rule). CI's `code` job skips docs-only commits, so a cards-only commit is checked locally only; `pnpm check` on the next code commit catches it.
- Ship ordering: the hash is unknown before the ship commit, so the log entry is appended before the commit and the hash filled in right after, with `## Ship` (step 9); the board is regenerated twice (before the ship commit and after the move to ship).
- Portability: docs.test.sh runs on macOS bash 3.2 and ubuntu bash 5 (python3 3.12 on the runner): basic regex only, `grep -o`, `cut -d'|'`, no `mapfile`, no `sed -i`, no `grep -P`; the plan-copies external diff is skipped on CI by design.
- E2 parsing assumes 4 cells per row and ids in the first cell (true for all 52 rows today); a future title containing `|` shifts cells and the test fails loudly with the row.
- README links resolve locally even for untracked files; the builder checks every named path with `git ls-files` (step 7).
- Biome: `biome.json` excludes docs, factory, .claude and tools/monitor, and Markdown and shell are not Biome languages, so `pnpm lint` is unaffected; verify with the `pnpm check` run.
- serve.py loads card.py through importlib: keep module-level code free of new side effects and keep `parse`/`task_doc` unchanged; serve.test.py proves it.
- The decisions log: an entry without a located source is left out (the Spec's rule); the keyword list of the `decisions` case names only the sourced entries above.
- The two monitor-owner constraints (no event from `status`, nothing under `.bridle/progress/`): card.test.py's `test_no_side_effects` and the reviewer's grep for `log.sh`/`.bridle` in the `status` code path.

### Leave alone
`Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `tools/entrypoint.sh`, `docs/ops/local-dev.md`, `tools/test/image.test.sh`, the P0-02.4 card; `tools/agent-hq/`; `tools/monitor/{mirror.py,monlib.py,serve.py,watch.sh,trace-hook.sh,trace_hook.py,index.html,log.sh,check.py,backfill.py,ops.sh}`; everything under `.bridle/` (monitor.json is linked, not edited); the two plan files outside the repository (read only); every other card (the board reads them, nothing writes them); `docs/requirements`, `docs/architecture`, `docs/ux`, `docs/decisions/0001` and `0002`; `.github/workflows/check.yml` (P0-09 may add the docs test to CI's docs job); `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/agents/*`; `tools/doctor.sh`, `tools/dev.sh`, `biome.json`, `package.json`; the content of either plan body.

The assertion was a pipe, `awk '<the Spec>' card | grep -q -F -- 'Pause note (foreman, 2026-11-04)'`, under `set -o pipefail`: `grep -q` leaves at its first match, awk is killed by SIGPIPE while it writes the Spec text after the note, and pipefail turns that into a failure. Run alone, three times: `pipeline exit 141 PIPESTATUS 141 0` (awk 141, grep 0, so the note was found). It began when the rulings paragraph was appended after the pause note: the Spec was 21,627 bytes when this was measured, in loop 2 (16,296 at HEAD; 26,336 when the last pass ran, with the "Ratifications and second rulings" paragraph as it then stood), and the note starts at byte 15,229. #### What this pass added to the tests

Only `tools/test/docs.test.sh` changed: 318 lines added, none removed or changed (`diff` of the handed-over file against the final one prints additions only), bash 3.2, POSIX tools and python3, no pipe into `grep -q`. Every block sits inside an existing case and is marked "Third test station" with its ruling. `tools/monitor/test/card.test.py` is unchanged: no loop touched `card.py`.
- `plan-copies` (rulings 8 and 13): the roadmap header is seven lines with one Origin line; that line holds the ruled date phrase and its six other statements and no "as it stood"; the sizes it gives are derived, not pinned twice: the body has the bytes the line says ("so 79,909 bytes"), the lines the line gives the origin, and one byte more than the origin's size.
- `git-facts` (rulings 8 and 13; skipped outside a git work tree and in a shallow clone, like the rest of the case): the commit the Origin line names exists; its time on the UTC+8 clock is the one the line gives; the roadmap file it holds has the line count and the size the line gives and no final newline. The body's content is compared with nothing new: the sha256 pin and the hand check stay as the Origin line describes them.
- `readme` (rulings 1, 11 and 6): the resume phrases, the work-package clause and its quoted source in SKILL.md, the one-watch condition, no "with status running or blocked"; each status the README names is in the usage line of `card.py move`; `STATIONS` has spec to review between backlog and ship; and a fixture of 20 cards, one at every station from spec to review with every one of the four statuses, each ordered before one backlog card, for which `card.py status --cards` must name the backlog card as next up.
- `readme` (ruling 3): the label's two phrases and no "one test per tooling card"; `tools/monitor/test` holds a test file; every file of `tools/test` names one card in its second line, no two the same; each named card is P0-11 (and then the file is `docs.test.sh`) or a P0-02 or P0-06 sub-card with a card file; every done P0-02 or P0-06 sub-card has its file. A sub-card's test file may precede its ship (proof 34).
- `board` (ruling 2): the rule in the README's several-sessions list and in the opening paragraph of `docs/plan/progress.md`, across line wraps; the usage text of `card.py` it comes from; no "touches a card", across line wraps, in any of six documents (`README.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `factory/README.md`, `factory/line.md`, `docs/plan/progress.md`, SKILL.md).
- `board` (ruling 4): the legend's "unknown" clause; while the directory in which row 7 of the roadmap's E4 table lands the runners does not exist, the E4.7 status is `unknown: ` with a reason. Once that directory exists the assertion stops binding (proof 41), so the card that adds the runners rewrites the cell without touching the test.
- `board` (ruling 10): the heading `## In flight / next / only the owner can do` exists by the name the three ship documents use; the shape paragraph describes the pause entries; every pause entry is `- Paused <date> <time> (<who>): ...`.
- `decisions` (ruling 5): the two ruled sentences of the intro; every dated row either carries the marker or names a document, a card or an ADR in its last cell; the intro may not define the marker as "no other written source" while marked rows also name a card or a document (five do).
- `factory-docs` (rulings 1 and 11): the resume phrases in `## Resume`, across line wraps, and no "with status running or blocked".
- `factory-docs` (ruling 10): the in-flight rewrite in the README's ship sentence, in the job cell and the exit cell of the ship row, and in step 6 between `## Log` and `Cards: <id> shipped`, in that order.
- `factory-docs` (rulings 6, 9 and 12): step 0's one-watch condition and six phrases of its account of the mirror write for both tools; `.bridle/monitor.json` holds the `slug`, the `url` and the two collections step 0 takes from it; the three phrases of `new`; step 7's two phrases; the Mirroring paragraph's two phrases and no unconditional "refuses the whole batch".

Each statement below was checked here against the file, git or the tool schema it names, and not taken from `## Build` or `## Test`. The Spec is met in the files as they stand, every criterion and every ruling that concerns a delivered file has a passing assertion, and nothing unrelated changed. No finding fails the card. #### Findings that fail the card

#### Advisory findings (none fails the card)

1. Test file, delivered, not one of today's additions. `tools/test/docs.test.sh`, case `factory-docs`, the assertion whose message is "the E1 section of docs/plan/roadmap.md does not hold the fixtures sentence the station agents quote": it is still `awk '<the E1 section>' "$ROADMAP" | grep -q -F -- "$Q"` under `set -o pipefail`, the shape that ratification (a) removed from two other assertions. On macOS awk writes the 4,607-byte section at once. On Linux it does not. In the `bridle:dev` image (mawk 1.3.4, GNU grep 3.8) the section leaves awk as 4,096 bytes and then 511: of 60 single reads of the pipe, 9 returned 4,096 bytes and 51 returned 4,607. The line that holds the sentence ends at byte 3,532, inside the first write. A reader that takes the first write before awk ends can leave, and awk then dies of SIGPIPE at the second: with `dd count=1` as the reader, awk ended at 141 in 14 of 200 runs. With `grep -q` itself, 1000 runs in the image and 300 on macOS: none with a 141 or any non-zero status, because awk ends before grep's first read. `gh run view` gives `conclusion: success` for the Actions runs on `8e1f6dd` and `8cdcf34` (both commits change files outside `docs/**`, `factory/**` and `*.md`, so by the workflow's filter the code job ran the assertion on ubuntu). So no false failure has been seen, and the pipe dates from the first test pass; but on CI's platform the result rests on timing, not on one write. Fix, plumbing only (same input, pattern and message), in the next change of this file: `awk '/^### E1[.]/{f=1;next} /^### E2[.]/{f=0} f' "$ROADMAP" > "$tmp/e1"`, then `grep -q -F -- "$Q" "$tmp/e1" || fail "the E1 section of docs/plan/roadmap.md does not hold the fixtures sentence the station agents quote"`. Why it does not fail the card: the suite passed in every run made here and by the tester, the assertion is neither new nor weakened, and a 141 would show as a false failure, never as a false pass.
2. Test file, delivered, added today by the second test pass. Case `decisions`, the assertion on the Spec's Why paragraph: `awk '<the Spec>' <the card> | grep '^Why: ' | grep -q -F -- ...`. A file-reading producer stands at the head of a pipeline that ends in `grep -q`, so by its shape it touches the rule of the foreman's brief ("no pipe from a file-reading producer into grep -q under pipefail"). As written it cannot give a 141: the middle `grep` reads awk's output to its end and prints one line (the Spec has exactly one line that starts with "Why: "), so nothing is written after `grep -q` leaves. Run alone under pipefail: 300 times on macOS and 1000 in the Linux image, no 141. I count the rule as met in substance and the assertion as sound. If the foreman wants the shape gone: write the Spec to `"$tmp/p0-11-spec"` at that place (the `pause-check` case writes the same file further down and can then reuse it), then `grep '^Why: ' "$tmp/p0-11-spec" > "$tmp/why" || true` and `grep -q -F -- "(\"$QI\")" "$tmp/why" || fail "<the same message>"`. The other four pipes into `grep -q` that today's work added are fed by a `printf` of one variable or by a `grep` over a few rows of a temporary file.
3. Working record. `## Build`, `### Loop 2`, C15 ("the `factory-docs` pipe over the roadmap's E1 section (4,607 bytes, awk's one write)") and `## Test`, third pass, A9 ("The largest input written before the match is the E1 section (4,607 bytes; ...); a pipe's `st_blksize` is 16,384 on this machine") are true of macOS only (finding 1). Exact text to add to each: "(on Linux, mawk 1.3.4 writes that section as 4,096 and then 511 bytes and the sentence's line ends at byte 3,532, so there the result rests on awk ending before grep's first read: no 141 in 1000 runs; second review, finding 1)". It misleads nobody about the state of the repository.
4. Working record. Spec, "Pause note (foreman, 2026-11-04)": its last sentence still reads "Resume: `/factory run P0-11` starting at the test station (the card is at build with status blocked; ...)", and `README.md`, `factory/README.md` and `CLAUDE.md` send a resuming session to the pause note of a parked card's Spec, while this card stands at review, running. I judge that it does not send a session wrong about the current state: the note is dated, it names the state it applies to (build, blocked), which the frontmatter no longer shows, and the paragraph right after it says the owner resumed the card on 2026-11-17. Exact text to append to the note: "Overtaken 2026-11-17: the card was resumed at the test station that day (next paragraph); its station is the one in the frontmatter." The assertion of `pause-check` pins only the note's title.
5. Working record. Spec, "Final pre-test check": "The delivered documents and `tools/test/docs.test.sh` are as the builder's last pass left them." Overtaken for the test file. Exact text to append: "(Overtaken for the test file: the third test pass added 318 lines to it and removed or changed none; the delivered documents are as the last pass left them.)"

#### Accepted interpretations

- Known and by design, as the foreman's brief says: the board row of P0-11 and the "In flight / next / only the owner can do" paragraphs of `docs/plan/progress.md` still describe the pause of 2026-11-04. The foreman regenerates the board and rewrites the paragraphs in the `Cards: P0-11 shipped` commit; the `board` case fails that commit otherwise (the tester's proofs 7 to 9, and M6 here for the phrase it binds on). The owner-attributed statements of 2026-11-04 (the six M0 rows "not started", due 2026-11-16; the six chat-only rows; the session names of the Paused entry) are records of that date, and the M0 rows are the owner's to update. The Origin line says "committed verbatim in 8e0e550" twice; both are true.
- `CLAUDE.md` says "the pause note at the end of its Spec", the two READMEs "the pause note of its Spec": ratification (c). In `8cdcf34`, when this card was parked, the pause note was the last paragraph of its Spec (read with `git show HEAD:<card>`), so `CLAUDE.md` was true of the only card that has been parked, and no card is parked now.
- The exempt list of the `roadmap-cards` case holds P0-12, one id more than AC6 names (the pause check's one line of the test). P0-12 is a tooling card opened by the owner's decision of 2026-11-04 (the last row of the decisions log), the class AC6 exempts, and without it the case fails on a card the owner asked for. The Spec does not say so yet. One clause for the foreman to add beside ratifications (a) to (d): "(e) the exempt list of `roadmap-cards` holds P0-12, added by the pause check (`8cdcf34`): a tooling card opened by the owner's decision of 2026-11-04, not an E2 row."
- SKILL.md step 0, "the foreman's session of 2026-11-17 mirrored card P0-11's whole second test station with it": it rests on the foreman's word (ruling 9); no tracked file proves or contradicts it.
- The new assertions tie this card's test to files of other sessions, read-only: `tools/monitor/trace_hook.py` (`target_of` is imported and called), `tools/monitor/README.md`, and `vite.config.ts`, `server/index.ts` and `.env.example` of `tools/agent-hq`. A change there that makes the trace sentence of SKILL.md or the port table untrue turns `pnpm check` red until the document follows. That is the statement being kept true, and the test says so at each place.
- The in-flight assertions of `board` bind on two phrases ("last shipped card is <id>", "<id> ... is parked"); a rewritten block in other words is not held by them. That is enough for ruling 10: the block as it stands uses both, so an unrewritten block fails the ship commit.
- "One screen" (AC2): 55 lines against the limit of 80, with step 2 of "How to resume" as one long line. `packages` and `crates` share one line of the map, as at the first review.
- "That path has since been reused" in the roadmap's Origin line rests on `## Test`, second pass, item 10. Nothing under the home plans directory was read here.

#### Boundaries

Nothing staged, committed or pushed. No file changed but this section of this card. Under `.bridle/` only this station's own `tools/monitor/log.sh` reports and the trace hook wrote (`.bridle/progress/factory/P0-11-review.jsonl` and `P0-11-review.trace.jsonl`). No artifact database tool was called: ToolSearch loaded the `ArtifactData` schema so that it could be read. Network: `gh run view` on this repository's two runs. Docker: three `docker run --rm --network none` of the local `bridle:dev` image with the repository mounted read-only (the pipes and the measurements of finding 1) and one of the local node image without a mount (to see that it has no python3), besides `docker images`, `docker ps` and `docker image inspect`, which only list; the development stack was not touched. git was used read-only on the real repository, plus one `clone` from it into the scratchpad, which is removed. The drivers and their outputs are in `p0-11-review2/` under the session scratchpad. Before they had that directory, this station's first outputs were written at the top of the scratchpad as `docs.out`, `docs.err`, `card.out`, `check.out`, `ws.out`, `regr.out`, `status.before`, `status.diff`, `boardcheck.out` and `pycache.out`; an earlier station's scratch output of the same name, if one stood there, was overwritten (nothing in the repository).

### First review (2026-11-04, before loop 1)

The first review's verdict was FAIL (this line read "VERDICT: FAIL" until the second review, and is reworded so that one line of the section starts with that word).

Reviewed 2026-11-04 (local clock UTC+8) by factory-reviewer on the working tree the tester left (HEAD `aac9cca`; 17 modified files, 5 new ones, nothing staged). Two statements of fact in the delivered documents are contradicted by the repository or recorded nowhere in it. This card's Spec exists so that a session does not invent state ("a session that cannot find the state will invent it"), and AC3 and AC4 require every fact to be sourced and checked against `git log`, never written from memory. The audit addendum asks for the stale lines to be fixed, not swapped for different wrong ones. So these two break the Spec, not just the style. Each needs a one-line fix.

Findings that fail the card:
1. `docs/ops/secrets.md:11`: "present in `.gitignore` since P0-02.1 (`bd758ed`)" is false. `git log --format=%h -S'.env.local' -- .gitignore` returns only `89bed6d` (PLAN-04, 2026-11-03 07:23). That commit added `.env.local` and `.env.*.local` with their comment line, and it is the same commit that wrote the stale "not there yet" sentence. `git show bd758ed -- .gitignore` adds only the build-output block (`node_modules/` to `*.tsbuildinfo`). The builder copied the audit addendum's wording exactly, so the Spec is wrong here, but the line still misstates the history it was meant to correct. Fix: write "present in `.gitignore` since PLAN-04 (`89bed6d`)" (the foreman amends the addendum, or the builder records the deviation in `## Build`) and change the pinned text at `tools/test/docs.test.sh:532` to match. Better still: have `git-facts` compare the cited hash with `git log --format=%h -S.env.local -- .gitignore | tail -n 1`, so the test proves the claim instead of only checking its wording.
2. `docs/plan/progress.md:117` (owner table, "M0 SME framework agreements signed"): the "Needed before" cell names "the P5-08 verification records", and no document records that. P5-08 (`docs/plan/roadmap.md:331`) is the content-pipeline milestone (deterministic-only grading in staging) and says nothing about SME records. E4.8 (`docs/plan/roadmap.md:362`), which the cell itself cites, gates "SME framework agreements and signed verification records" on "P2-11, the P2-08 clause attestation, P7-04 and P7-05". P7-04 (`:342`) is the row where the templates are "SME-signed". Fix: "the P2-08 clause attestation and P7-04's SME-signed templates (E4.8)", or copy E4.8's gate list.

Findings to fix in the same loop (both are about truth; neither fails the card alone):
3. `docs/plan/progress.md:124-125` with `:140`: the log's rule "The hash is the one in the card's `## Ship` section" does not hold for the P0-06 entry. P0-06's `## Ship` is empty. The P0-01 entry (`:128`) and the PLAN-05 entry (`:135`) say where their hashes come from; this one does not. Its `dfd0b72` comes from the audit addendum and from P0-06's history line 2026-11-03T10:43:19Z, which is `dfd0b72`'s commit time. The foreman's planned fill of that `## Ship` ("its sub-cards' commits") would not add `dfd0b72` either. Fix: add one clause to the entry ("its `## Ship` is empty; `dfd0b72` carries its done state, history 2026-11-03T10:43:19Z"), or have the foreman name `dfd0b72` in P0-06's `## Ship` at ship.
4. This card, `## Build` deviation 9 (line 141): "not to C8, which does not say it" is false. C8 (`docs/plan/roadmap.md:166`) reads "Formative tasks (UX question 9): instructors do not review formative grades". The decisions-log row (`docs/decisions/log.md:23`) is still correctly sourced, because ADR 0001 R11 and `docs/ux/ux-spec.md` open question 9 both hold the rule. The builder corrects the deviation note and may add C8 line 166 to that row's citations.

Advisory (does not fail the card):
5. `.claude/skills/factory/SKILL.md:69-75` with `tools/test/docs.test.sh:600-608`: step 6 appends the log entry before the `<id>: <title>` commit, with "the hash after the commit". In a full work tree, `git-facts` fails any `- <date> <time>, ...` entry that has no 7-hex `commit <hash>`, or whose time differs from the commit's. CI's shallow checkout skips those checks; a local `pnpm check` on that commit does not. Either say that the provisional entry carries the date only (no time, no `commit` field), or append the entry in the `Cards: <id> shipped` commit.
6. `README.md:43`: SKILL.md step 0 requires roadmap approval only for P1 and later cards, and it arms the watch after the preconditions, not as one of them. The README's wording follows the Plan's step 7.
7. `docs/plan/progress.md:120`: architecture section 17 says R2 owns "the pack and conformance work so steps 3 and 5 overlap". That work belongs to step 5 and overlaps step 3; it is not "the work of steps 3 and 5". The `(P2-06 to P2-08)` mapping is fine.
8. `docs/plan/progress.md:124`: the shape line gives `- <date>, <id>, ...` and "one entry per shipped card", but the entries carry `<date> <time>` and three of them are "no card" commits. The PLAN-01 to PLAN-04 and P0-04 entries use the commit subjects' titles, not the card titles. Both are true, just not what the shape line says.
9. `docs/decisions/log.md:25`: "the instruction itself: chat only", while the same row cites the P0-11 Spec, which quotes the instruction. "before more product code" paraphrases "prioritize this".

Checked and found fine: the plan copies. Their bodies are `cmp`-identical to `8e0e550`, plus only the roadmap's final newline, and `git diff` shows only the 7 and 5 header lines. The byte and line counts are 79,908 bytes / 371 lines and 21,663 bytes / 109 lines, with the Plan's sha256 prefixes. The header facts hold: roadmap lines 20 and 22 (second approval; the first covered Part D only), D2 to D6 present, line 36 ("PLAN-05 sits at review"), `0155881` at 07:23, and P0-10's "plan approved 2026-11-03" and `126d1f8`. Also fine: `tools/monitor/card.py`. Its `list` output is byte-identical to HEAD's on the real cards (72 lines). Outside comments, the `status` path names no subprocess, `LOG`, clock, environment or `.bridle`. `--write` touches only the named file, is idempotent and creates nothing under `.bridle/`. `--check` compares sets and prints nothing when the board is current. `--write` with `--check` is refused with exit 2, and no bytecode is written. The other doc fixes each change only their own line: the build-sequence and 00-index pointers; secrets.md's E4 pointer; the MVP-02 line, inside MVP-02 and matching ADR 0001 R1 and ADR 0002 A2/A6; ADR 0002's file-08 list; and 68 in `docs/ux/README.md`, equal to the inventory's 68 `yes` rows. Also fine: `tools/check.sh` step 7 and every 6-to-7 pin in `tools/test/workspace.test.sh`. All 28 cited hashes exist, and every log time matches `git log` at UTC+8. Every decisions row matches its source: roadmap lines 14, 20, 22, 30-34, 49, 166, 168, 347 and 351-363; ADR 0002's status line, A3 and A9; ADR 0001 R11; ux-spec open question 9; P0-06 history 08:33:53Z; P0-02.2 history 11:52:30Z and `compose.yaml`; `docs/ops/permissions.md:45`; `docs/ops/github.md` items (a), (d) and (e); the monitor-v4 Decisions section; `.bridle/monitor.json` `syncIntervalSeconds: 5`; and P0-10's `## Build`. The README checks out: its opening comes from roadmap line 12 and the Stack row, the calendar from ADR 0002 A4, 14 documents / 517 requirements match the index, every link resolves and is tracked or new, both anchors are headings, `tools/agent-hq` is outside `pnpm-workspace.yaml` and excluded from Biome, and serve.py binds 127.0.0.1:8787. The owner table's items and Needed-before cells match E4 rows 1 to 9, and its six M0 items match section 17. Its statuses hold: github.md items, doctor's 11 tools, secrets.md "Obtained at" P0-05.2, P0-07 at backlog. Nothing outside the Spec's file list (with the addendum's additions) changed: no other card, no P0-02.4 file, no file of the other session. There are no secrets and no dead code, and no errors are swallowed: every `|| true` in the tests is followed by a count or emptiness check. Re-run: `bash tools/test/docs.test.sh` 9 ok, exit 0; `python3 tools/monitor/test/card.test.py` 11 OK; serve 2, check 7 and mirror 14 OK; `pnpm check` `check: 7/7 ok in 6.4s`; `bash tools/test/workspace.test.sh` 4 ok, exit 0. `git status --porcelain` was unchanged and no `__pycache__` was left after any of them.

Accepted interpretations:
- `--check` compares only the id set and the done set (audit addendum). The committed board shows P0-11 at `build`/`factory-builder` while the card is at `review`; that passes by design, and the foreman's `--write` at ship brings it current.
- `plan-copies` pins the committed bodies by sha256 and reads nothing under the home plans directory (the foreman's change). I did not read the origin files either; `8e0e550`'s byte counts and sha256 prefixes match the Plan's figures.
- The roadmap header is 7 lines, so every line citation shifts by 7. SKILL.md says "the plan file outside the repository is only its origin" instead of the literal path.
- `Dockerfile` is linked (tracked since `d8d8b7e`). `tools/agent-hq/` is named without a link, as committed in `aac9cca`.
- The three "no card" log entries were the foreman's request.
- The two agent files changed although the Plan's leave-alone list names `.claude/agents/*`; the audit addendum adds them.
- Step 6's "push and record the Actions run URL" repeats line.md's existing ship criterion.
- `card.test.py` has 11 tests and `docs.test.sh` 9 cases, against the Plan's 4 and 6.

For the loop: findings 1 to 4 are text edits in `docs/ops/secrets.md`, `docs/plan/progress.md`, `tools/test/docs.test.sh` (the pin at :532) and this card's `## Build`. Afterwards, re-run `bash tools/test/docs.test.sh` and `pnpm check`.

## Ship
